{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Adversarially review SIGIL Phase 8 WS-F \"SIGIL-HAND\" (branch phase8-ws-f-gesture at /home/kali/sigil) — a hand-gesture control system that injects cursor/keyboard input. This is the highest-risk surface: it drives the OS input devices. Attack its trust claims with concrete repros; report only substantiated findings, most severe first. Read the files fully.\n\nFILES:\n- kernel/src/tiers.rs (the WARDEN input-table change: INPUT_A1/INPUT_A2 exact-name tables + classify branch)\n- sigil/gesture/session.py (SessionGate — THE KEYSTONE: armed session + per-intent tier gate + inject-or-queue)\n- sigil/gesture/pipeline.py (GesturePipeline — the debounced fail-safe FSM)\n- sigil/gesture/features.py (invariant features + RuleClassifier)\n- sigil/gesture/{types,components,landmark,run}.py, sigil/perception/camera_stream.py\n- sigil/platform/input.py (per-OS InputBackend) + sigil/platform/base.py/linux.py + sigil/mesh/registry.py (_CAP_CORE signed flags)\n- tests/test_gesture.py (greenwashed?)\n\nCLAIM 1 (HIGHEST) — a gesture can NEVER trigger an unintended gated action; discrete A2 actions (type/combo/app.launch) ALWAYS queue and NEVER auto-inject; only reversible A1 pointer input injects, and only inside a live armed session. Attack:\n- Any path where SessionGate.handle injects an A2/A3 tool? Where the tier is mis-derived so type/launch inject? Where an unknown intent injects? Trace INTENT_TOOL + the tier check + _inject.\n- Can injection happen with NO live session (session None / disarmed / hand-lost)? After disarm, does a stale Session object still authorize? \n- Fail-safe on ambiguity: fuzz the FSM with rapid adversarial label flips / low-margin / low-confidence readings — can a discrete action fire without confirm_frames consecutive high-confidence high-margin frames? Can cooldown be bypassed to fire repeatedly?\n\nCLAIM 2 — the WARDEN oracle change does not weaken any existing gate; danger always wins; token-named tools are unaffected. Attack:\n- Does the FULL existing tiers.rs suite still pass? Does `hid.pointer.delete`/`hid.pointer.sudo` → A3 (danger-first before the input tables)? Does `file.move`/`data.type`/`content.type` stay A3 (the exact-name approach must NOT leak to token-named tools)? Any `hid.*` name that should be A3 but gets A1/A2? Does the raise-only registry still only tighten?\n- Is `hid.key.*` correctly NOT A1/A2 (the `key` token is an A3 secret-target → must be A3)? Verify hid.type/hid.combo avoid the collision.\n\nCLAIM 3 — structural: only the SessionGate (holding a valid armed-session token) can inject; injection is impossible outside a session; the session is owner-signed + bounded; movement is telemetry not per-frame spine records. Attack:\n- Is injection truly gated, or is the InputBackend object directly callable (bypassing the gate)? Is that a real bypass or convention? \n- Session token: can a stale/replayed session_id re-authorize after disarm? Is the arm record actually owner-SIGNED (verify signed_payload)? Is there any session expiry/bound, or can an armed session live forever?\n- Does the loop write a WARDEN/spine record per movement frame (a DoS on the append-only spine at 30fps)? Confirm only arm/disarm + discrete actions are logged.\n- assert_no_offense at import? The A2 queued action — is it truly withheld (no injection) and is there any auto-execute path that fires it without a verified approval?\n\nFor each objection: file:line, concrete repro, severity, fix hint. If a claim holds, say what you tried. Return findings; do not fix."},"uuid":"533461d2-0685-43ee-b3dd-354ab4217c06","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:31:39.793Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"533461d2-0685-43ee-b3dd-354ab4217c06","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"<command-message>crucible</command-message>\n<command-name>crucible</command-name>\n<skill-format>true</skill-format>"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main/.claude/skills/crucible\n\n# CRUCIBLE — operating the engine\n\nCRUCIBLE turns an authorized seed URL into **oracle-confirmed** findings, reasons over them,\nand produces auditable reports — inside a fail-closed safety stack. Its whole value is one\nrule; internalize it before anything else:\n\n> **A claim is a FACT only when a deterministic oracle fires over data a real target\n> produced.** You (the LLM) ADVISE where to look and what a result might mean; the oracle\n> CONFIRMS. Nothing else — not your confidence, not a critic's endorsement, not a plausible\n> story — promotes a claim to a fact.\n\nThe standing doctrine every reasoning call operates under is\n[framework/cognitive/metacognition.md](../../../framework/cognitive/metacognition.md) (it is\ninjected into every LLM system prompt). The full architecture — agents, event spine, veracity\nlayer, learning/metacognition core, safety stack — is\n[framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md](../../../framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md). The binding\nconstitution is [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md); never relax scope, destruction, evidence, or\nhonesty rules.\n\n## The invariants (never violate these)\n\n1. **Oracle authority.** Only a fired oracle confirms. Critics/RL/reflection/self-consistency\n   may only *advise, re-rank, defer, or abstain* — never promote a finding or override the oracle.\n2. **Coverage.** Never silently skip an authorized attack surface. Learning and reflection\n   *deprioritise* (re-rank / defer); they never gate a surface out.\n3. **Prove by re-execution.** A finding is a fact only if its retained `oracle_context`\n   re-fires. The veracity firewall (`framework/v2/veracity/`) re-runs proofs; it can only demote.\n4. **Non-circular learning.** An EXPLOITABLE label needs ≥2 independent corroborating oracle\n   kinds; a silent oracle is never auto-labelled a false positive.\n5. **Refuse honestly, fail closed.** Decline to conclude what you cannot ground; record every\n   refusal as evidence. Hard limits (scope, authorization, destruction, real user data) are\n   inviolable — when unsure, stop and ask.\n6. **Determinism + append-only.** No wallclock/global-rng in learning/reward math; the event\n   spine is append-only (supersede, never edit).\n\n## Structured workflows\n\n### Run an authorized engagement (end-to-end, gated)\n1. Confirm the active target's `targets/<slug>/charter.md` authorizes it (constitution §II).\n2. `python3 -m framework.v2 engage <slug> <seed-url> [--recon] [--spine]`\n   — crawls, audits, confirms via oracles, chains attack paths, scores confidence, and\n   (with `--spine`) mirrors the whole run onto the immutable blackboard event stream.\n   (`--strict-evidence` is a **`scan`**-only flag, below — not an `engage` flag.)\n3. Every request passes the full gate chain; an out-of-scope seed or tripped kill-switch\n   refuses *before* traffic and is recorded as a `refusal` event.\n\n### Loopback-only quick scan\n`python3 -m framework.v2 scan http://127.0.0.1:<port>/ --format json|sarif|html [--strict-evidence]`\n— the export states each finding's **live grounding** (fact / ungrounded / contradicted);\n`--strict-evidence` withholds non-fact findings from the rendered doc but keeps them in\n`--reverifiable-out`.\n\n### Re-verify a report (prove-don't-guess, offline)\n`python3 -m framework.v2 verify <report.json>` re-runs each finding's retained `oracle_context`.\n`python3 -m framework.v2 evidence ...` builds/verifies signed, hash-linked evidence certificates\n(and, with report claims bound in, fails closed when a report sentence has no backing evidence).\n\n### Inspect / audit the event spine\nThe blackboard (`framework/v2/agents/blackboard.py`) is the one append-only, typed, provenance-\nlinked event stream. Replay it with `Blackboard.replay(engagement=..., since_id=...)`.\nCryptographic tamper-evidence: `framework/v2/agents/spine_chain.py`\n(`build_spine_chain` / `sign_spine_head` / `verify_spine_head`).\n\n### Recon / intel, memory, self-improvement\n`python3 -m framework.v2 intel ...` (OSINT into the shared world-model; gated egress),\n`memory ...` (cross-engagement priors — never fabricate a score), `improve ...`\n(authorise-not-apply — it never self-applies a change).\n\n### Read-only operator console\n`python3 -m framework.v2 console` — loopback, read-only, zero-impact operator UI over the run.\n\n## What NOT to do\n- Do not build detection-evasion / identity-rotation / stay-hidden-from-defenders capability —\n  CRUCIBLE is correlatable and authorized by doctrine (constitution §VI).\n- Do not let any learned/critic/LLM signal enter the deterministic oracle/SCE/calibration inputs\n  or promote a finding.\n- Do not attack third parties (payment/IdP/CDN) — test only the operator's integration with them.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"uuid":"e9bf6e7c-fd43-4759-aa18-6ecdbf7ee6aa","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:31:39.805Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"98c0c379-7faa-4f45-ac1f-5a8f63c03e15","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SJRJnkazy67Svfg3cLnh1z","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SessionGate (Phase 8, WS-F, THE KEYSTONE) — the ONLY authority that injects input, and only inside\n2\tan owner-armed session. Two enforcement layers so a gesture can NEVER trigger an unintended action:\n3\t\n4\t  Layer 1 — the SESSION: arming appends a signed `gesture.session_armed` record (the proof-of-\n5\t  authorization, indicator-lit); a `session_id` is the only token that authorizes injection; disarm /\n6\t  hand-loss / expiry ends it. Injection is REFUSED with no live session.\n7\t\n8\t  Layer 2 — per intent, the tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`hid.*` names): A1 (pointer\n9\t  move/click/scroll/drag) injects within the session; A2 (type/combo/app.launch) is QUEUED for a\n10\t  verified owner/device approval bound to `sha256(session_id|tool|args)` and NEVER auto-injected — so\n11\t  a gesture alone can never type a password or launch an app.\n12\t\n13\tMovement is session-scoped telemetry (only arm/disarm + discrete actions hit the spine — per-frame\n14\tpointer records at 30 FPS would DoS the append-only log).\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\timport uuid\n18\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n19\tfrom typing import Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..agents.base import Tier\n22\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n23\tfrom .components import InputBackend\n24\tfrom .types import GestureIntent\n25\t\n26\tSESSION_ARMED = \"gesture.session_armed\"\n27\tSESSION_DISARMED = \"gesture.session_disarmed\"\n28\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"gesture.action\"\n29\t\n30\tINTENT_TOOL = {\n31\t    \"move\": \"hid.pointer.move\", \"click\": \"hid.pointer.click\",\n32\t    \"scroll_left\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\", \"scroll_right\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\",\n33\t    \"drag\": \"hid.pointer.drag\", \"type\": \"hid.type\", \"combo\": \"hid.combo\", \"launch\": \"hid.app.launch\",\n34\t}\n35\t\n36\t\n37\t@dataclass\n38\tclass Session:\n39\t    session_id: str\n40\t    live: bool = True\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tclass SessionGate:\n44\t    def __init__(self, store, backend: InputBackend, *, classifier=None, owner_key=None):\n45\t        self.store = store\n46\t        self.backend = backend\n47\t        self._classifier = classifier\n48\t        self._owner_key = owner_key\n49\t        self.session: Optional[Session] = None\n50\t\n51\t    def _cls(self):\n52\t        if self._classifier is None:\n53\t            from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n54\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n55\t        return self._classifier\n56\t\n57\t    # --- Layer 1: the owner-armed session ---------------------------------------------------------\n58\t    def arm(self, *, owner_key=None) -> Session:\n59\t        from ..governor.authn import signed_payload\n60\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair\n61\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else (self._owner_key or owner_keypair())\n62\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n63\t        core = {\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid}\n64\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n65\t                          payload={**signed_payload(core, ok), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n66\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED (indicator lit — opt-in)\"})\n67\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True)\n68\t        return self.session\n69\t\n70\t    def disarm(self) -> None:\n71\t        if self.session is not None:\n72\t            self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n73\t                              payload={\"signal\": SESSION_DISARMED, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n74\t                                       \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"summary\": \"gesture session DISARMED\"})\n75\t            self.session.live = False\n76\t        self.session = None\n77\t\n78\t    # --- Layer 2: per-intent tier gate ------------------------------------------------------------\n79\t    def handle(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> dict:\n80\t        \"\"\"Authorize + act on ONE intent. Returns a verdict dict. Injection ONLY inside a live session\n81\t        and ONLY for A1 tools; A2+ queues for approval and injects NOTHING; a danger name → refused.\"\"\"\n82\t        if intent.kind == \"hand_lost\":\n83\t            self.disarm()\n84\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"hand lost — session disarmed\"}\n85\t        if self.session is None or not self.session.live:\n86\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"no armed session — injection refused\"}\n87\t        tool = INTENT_TOOL.get(intent.kind)\n88\t        if tool is None:\n89\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": f\"unknown intent {intent.kind}\"}\n90\t        tier = self._cls().classify(tool)                 # DERIVED, fail-closed A3 on any error\n91\t        args = f\"{intent.dx:.4f},{intent.dy:.4f},{intent.arg}\"\n92\t        if tier <= Tier.A1:\n93\t            self._inject(intent)\n94\t            return {\"injected\": True, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool}\n95\t        # A2/A3 → QUEUE an approval bound to this exact action; inject NOTHING\n96\t        token = sha256_hex(f\"{self.session.session_id}|{tool}|{args}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n97\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"PERCEPTION\",\n98\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"queued\",\n99\t                                         \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n100\t                                         \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token, \"args\": args,\n101\t                                         \"subject\": f\"gesture action {tool} (awaiting approval)\"})\n102\t        return {\"injected\": False, \"queued\": seq, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token}\n103\t\n104\t    def _inject(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> None:\n105\t        b = self.backend\n106\t        if intent.kind == \"move\" or intent.kind == \"drag\":\n107\t            b.move(intent.dx, intent.dy)\n108\t        elif intent.kind == \"click\":\n109\t            b.click()\n110\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_left\":\n111\t            b.scroll(-1, 0)\n112\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_right\":\n113\t            b.scroll(1, 0)\n114\t"}]},"uuid":"db879024-33f1-4dc0-ae5a-42f034db77de","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:04.403Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"98c0c379-7faa-4f45-ac1f-5a8f63c03e15","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"da64e7be-3366-49ee-8cd5-050fdc739e51","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QJcV1gjUXvJ8fXnmp3j9ou","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"GesturePipeline (Phase 8, WS-F) — a frame-driven, debounced intent FSM, a structural copy of\n2\t`voice/pipeline.py`: timing in FRAME COUNTS (not wallclock), hysteresis so one noisy frame fires\n3\tNOTHING. `on_frame(reading) -> Optional[GestureIntent]`. Fail-safe by construction:\n4\t\n5\t  • a POINT pose drives continuous pointer `move` intents (deadzone-filtered);\n6\t  • a discrete gesture (pinch→click, swipe→scroll, spread→launch, twofinger→type) fires ONLY after\n7\t    `confirm_frames` consecutive identical HIGH-confidence, HIGH-margin readings, then a cooldown;\n8\t  • low confidence OR a small top1−top2 margin ⇒ NEUTRAL ⇒ counter resets ⇒ nothing fires (ambiguity\n9\t    does nothing);\n10\t  • `hand_lost_frames` of no-hand/neutral ⇒ a `hand_lost` intent so the session AUTO-DISARMS.\n11\t\n12\tThe FSM decides INTENT; authorization (a live armed session + the WARDEN tier) is the SessionGate's\n13\tjob — so this stays a pure, deterministically-testable state machine.\"\"\"\n14\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n15\t\n16\tfrom typing import Optional\n17\t\n18\tfrom .types import GestureIntent, GestureReading\n19\t\n20\t# discrete gesture label → intent kind (a POINT pose is the continuous move driver, handled separately)\n21\tDISCRETE = {\n22\t    \"pinch\": \"click\", \"swipe_left\": \"scroll_left\", \"swipe_right\": \"scroll_right\",\n23\t    \"spread\": \"launch\", \"twofinger\": \"type\", \"grab\": \"drag\",\n24\t}\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tclass GesturePipeline:\n28\t    def __init__(self, *, confirm_frames: int = 4, cooldown_frames: int = 6, hand_lost_frames: int = 8,\n29\t                 conf_min: float = 0.6, margin_min: float = 0.15, deadzone: float = 0.002):\n30\t        self.confirm_frames = confirm_frames\n31\t        self.cooldown_frames = cooldown_frames\n32\t        self.hand_lost_frames = hand_lost_frames\n33\t        self.conf_min = conf_min\n34\t        self.margin_min = margin_min\n35\t        self.deadzone = deadzone\n36\t        self._label: Optional[str] = None\n37\t        self._count = 0\n38\t        self._cool = 0\n39\t        self._lost = 0\n40\t\n41\t    def on_frame(self, reading: Optional[GestureReading]) -> Optional[GestureIntent]:\n42\t        if self._cool > 0:\n43\t            self._cool -= 1\n44\t        # no hand / explicit neutral → count toward auto-disarm; a discrete streak is broken\n45\t        if reading is None or not reading.label or reading.label == \"neutral\":\n46\t            self._label, self._count = None, 0\n47\t            self._lost += 1\n48\t            if self._lost >= self.hand_lost_frames:\n49\t                self._lost = 0\n50\t                return GestureIntent(\"hand_lost\")\n51\t            return None\n52\t        self._lost = 0\n53\t        # AMBIGUITY → nothing (fail-safe): a single noisy or low-margin frame cannot fire\n54\t        if reading.confidence < self.conf_min or reading.margin < self.margin_min:\n55\t            self._label, self._count = None, 0\n56\t            return None\n57\t        if reading.label == \"point\":                       # continuous pointer control\n58\t            self._label, self._count = None, 0\n59\t            if abs(reading.dx) < self.deadzone and abs(reading.dy) < self.deadzone:\n60\t                return None\n61\t            return GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=reading.dx, dy=reading.dy)\n62\t        # a discrete gesture: require `confirm_frames` consecutive identical readings (hysteresis)\n63\t        if reading.label == self._label:\n64\t            self._count += 1\n65\t        else:\n66\t            self._label, self._count = reading.label, 1\n67\t        if self._count >= self.confirm_frames and self._cool == 0:\n68\t            self._count = 0\n69\t            self._cool = self.cooldown_frames\n70\t            kind = DISCRETE.get(reading.label, \"none\")\n71\t            return GestureIntent(kind, arg=reading.label) if kind != \"none\" else None\n72\t        return None\n73\t"}]},"uuid":"24c4a6eb-ac68-40df-960e-c670add1eaff","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:04.578Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"da64e7be-3366-49ee-8cd5-050fdc739e51","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6322838b-8f13-4929-a400-6c812a378521","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DX8CF1Zru6Csi6kL1mTVjz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Landmark features + a from-foundation classifier (Phase 8, WS-F F4). `invariant_features` turns 21\n2\traw keypoints into a TRANSLATION/SCALE/ROTATION-invariant vector: recenter to the wrist (kp0), scale\n3\tby the wrist→middle-MCP span (kp9), canonicalize rotation to that axis. Invariance is a hard,\n4\ttestable property (translate/scale/rotate the same hand → identical features) and is what makes the\n5\tclassifier robust. `RuleClassifier` is a working baseline over the features (extended-finger counting\n6\t+ pinch distance); a trained MLP/TCN over these features (exported ONNX/npz, `train/` offline) is the\n7\tdocumented upgrade behind the same `GestureModel` Protocol.\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport math\n11\tfrom typing import List\n12\t\n13\tfrom .types import GestureReading, Hand\n14\t\n15\t_TIPS = (4, 8, 12, 16, 20)\n16\t_PIPS = (2, 6, 10, 14, 18)\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef _dist(a, b) -> float:\n20\t    return math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1])\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef invariant_features(hand: Hand) -> List[float]:\n24\t    pts = [(lm[0], lm[1]) for lm in hand.landmarks]\n25\t    if len(pts) < 21:\n26\t        return []\n27\t    wx, wy = pts[0]\n28\t    rel = [(x - wx, y - wy) for x, y in pts]              # translation-invariant (recenter to wrist)\n29\t    span = math.hypot(rel[9][0], rel[9][1]) or 1e-6\n30\t    rel = [(x / span, y / span) for x, y in rel]          # scale-invariant (wrist→mid-MCP span)\n31\t    theta = math.atan2(rel[9][1], rel[9][0])\n32\t    cos, sin = math.cos(-theta), math.sin(-theta)\n33\t    rot = [(x * cos - y * sin, x * sin + y * cos) for x, y in rel]   # rotation-canonicalized\n34\t    out: List[float] = []\n35\t    for x, y in rot:\n36\t        out += [x, y]\n37\t    return out\n38\t\n39\t\n40\tclass RuleClassifier:\n41\t    \"\"\"A working, dependency-free static-pose classifier over the invariant geometry.\"\"\"\n42\t    def classify(self, hands: List[Hand]) -> GestureReading:\n43\t        if not hands:\n44\t            return GestureReading(\"neutral\", 0.0)\n45\t        h = hands[0]\n46\t        pts = [(lm[0], lm[1]) for lm in h.landmarks]\n47\t        if len(pts) < 21:\n48\t            return GestureReading(\"neutral\", 0.0)\n49\t        wrist = pts[0]\n50\t        span = _dist(pts[0], pts[9]) or 1e-6\n51\t        extended = [_dist(pts[t], wrist) > _dist(pts[p], wrist) for t, p in zip(_TIPS, _PIPS)]\n52\t        n = sum(extended)\n53\t        pinch = _dist(pts[4], pts[8]) < 0.35 * span       # thumb-tip near index-tip\n54\t        if pinch and n <= 2:\n55\t            return GestureReading(\"pinch\", 0.9, margin=0.5)\n56\t        if n == 0:\n57\t            return GestureReading(\"fist\", 0.9, margin=0.5)\n58\t        if n >= 4:\n59\t            return GestureReading(\"open\", 0.9, margin=0.5)\n60\t        if extended[1] and n == 1:                        # index only → point (pointer driver)\n61\t            return GestureReading(\"point\", 0.9, dx=0.0, dy=0.0, margin=0.4)\n62\t        return GestureReading(\"neutral\", 0.3, margin=0.1)\n63\t"}]},"uuid":"11236a86-ab03-4cbc-91fe-e9f4fea53c0f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:04.692Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6322838b-8f13-4929-a400-6c812a378521","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"27e4f1ea-1bc4-4c20-97df-bf621740ccea","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a970854d297d55219","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAEkBpYwATeDePkXWnGu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VPNfaHTYfgF78eA4sTzBjJ","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/kernel/src/tiers.rs"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5503,"cache_read_input_tokens":26423,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5503,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAEkAZtq71HGtS4YaQU4","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"e39abe71-78d9-40b9-8768-fd1455128ebe","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:09.653Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"e39abe71-78d9-40b9-8768-fd1455128ebe","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VPNfaHTYfgF78eA4sTzBjJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! WARDEN autonomy tiers (SIGIL §5). Every tool invocation is classified A0–A3; the gate\n2\t//! decision follows from the tier. FAIL-CLOSED and TOKEN-BASED: the tool name is split into\n3\t//! whole tokens (on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/`/space) and matched against whole-token sets — never a raw\n4\t//! substring, so \"overwrite\" is not \"write\" and \"forget\" is not \"get\". Danger is checked FIRST,\n5\t//! and A0 (auto, un-queued) is reachable ONLY via a positive safe-verb allowlist; anything not\n6\t//! positively classified — including any unknown or dangerous-target tool — is A3.\n7\t\n8\tuse std::fmt;\n9\t\n10\tuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n11\t\n12\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n13\tpub enum Tier {\n14\t    /// Observe / answer — memory queries, screen read, voice reply. Auto.\n15\t    A0,\n16\t    /// Reversible internal act — write a report/brief, draft-branch, raise an alert. Auto + logged.\n17\t    A1,\n18\t    /// External-visible / semi-reversible — send email, calendar write, purchase < cap. Queued.\n19\t    A2,\n20\t    /// Destructive / financial / security — push protected branch, deploy, delete, spend > cap. Explicit.\n21\t    A3,\n22\t}\n23\t\n24\timpl Tier {\n25\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n26\t        match self {\n27\t            Tier::A0 => \"A0\",\n28\t            Tier::A1 => \"A1\",\n29\t            Tier::A2 => \"A2\",\n30\t            Tier::A3 => \"A3\",\n31\t        }\n32\t    }\n33\t}\n34\t\n35\timpl fmt::Display for Tier {\n36\t    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n37\t        f.write_str(self.as_str())\n38\t    }\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n42\tpub enum Decision {\n43\t    Auto,\n44\t    Queued,\n45\t    ExplicitRequired,\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\timpl Decision {\n49\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n50\t        match self {\n51\t            Decision::Auto => \"auto\",\n52\t            Decision::Queued => \"queued\",\n53\t            Decision::ExplicitRequired => \"explicit-required\",\n54\t        }\n55\t    }\n56\t    pub fn may_run(&self) -> bool {\n57\t        matches!(self, Decision::Auto)\n58\t    }\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t/// A3 — destructive verbs, financial ops, crypto/restore ops, and DANGEROUS TARGETS\n62\t/// (secret/identity/network/prod material is A3 regardless of the verb: `secrets.read`,\n63\t/// `iam.policy.write`, `budget.get`, `vault.read`).\n64\tconst A3_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n65\t    // destructive verbs\n66\t    \"push\", \"deploy\", \"delete\", \"destroy\", \"drop\", \"remove\", \"purge\", \"wipe\", \"truncate\",\n67\t    \"overwrite\", \"erase\", \"format\", \"kill\", \"shutdown\", \"reboot\", \"reset\", \"disable\", \"enable\",\n68\t    \"override\", \"force\", \"sudo\", \"exec\", \"eval\", \"chmod\", \"chown\", \"patch\", \"install\", \"uninstall\",\n69\t    // crypto + restore/rollback (a restore silently reverts current state)\n70\t    \"encrypt\", \"decrypt\", \"restore\", \"revert\", \"rollback\", \"recover\",\n71\t    // financial\n72\t    \"spend\", \"purchase\", \"pay\", \"payment\", \"transaction\", \"transfer\", \"refund\", \"invoice\",\n73\t    \"allocate\", \"budget\", \"release\", \"sign\",\n74\t    // security / identity / infra / secret-store TARGETS (dangerous no matter the verb)\n75\t    \"secret\", \"secrets\", \"credential\", \"credentials\", \"token\", \"tokens\", \"key\", \"keys\", \"iam\",\n76\t    \"policy\", \"firewall\", \"acl\", \"role\", \"grant\", \"revoke\", \"rotate\", \"escalate\", \"infra\",\n77\t    \"prod\", \"production\", \"env\", \"master\", \"root\", \"admin\",\n78\t    \"vault\", \"keyring\", \"keychain\", \"keystore\", \"hsm\",\n79\t];\n80\t\n81\t/// A2 — external-visible / semi-reversible: communication, publishing, and bulk DATA EGRESS\n82\t/// (export/dump of a private store must be human-seen, never auto).\n83\tconst A2_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n84\t    \"send\", \"email\", \"smtp\", \"publish\", \"post\", \"message\", \"outbound\", \"webhook\", \"sms\", \"notify\",\n85\t    \"share\", \"upload\", \"invite\", \"calendar\", \"tweet\", \"dm\", \"export\", \"dump\", \"download\", \"sync\",\n86\t];\n87\t\n88\t/// A1 — reversible internal writes (drafts, notes, memory writes, non-push commits). NOTE:\n89\t/// \"snapshot\" is intentionally NOT here — a snapshot RESTORE is destructive (caught by A3\n90\t/// \"restore\"), and snapshot-create alone falls to fail-closed A3 rather than risk auto-run.\n91\tconst A1_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n92\t    \"write\", \"note\", \"brief\", \"report\", \"draft\", \"alert\", \"consolidate\", \"commit\", \"branch\",\n93\t    \"annotate\", \"tag\", \"label\",\n94\t];\n95\t\n96\t/// A0 — POSITIVE allowlist of known-safe observe/answer VERBS only (NO target nouns — a target\n97\t/// noun here would let e.g. `memory.encrypt` auto-run). A tool reaches A0 only by a read-verb\n98\t/// here, or an exact name in `A0_TOOLS`; absence of danger alone is NOT sufficient → fail-closed A3.\n99\tconst A0_VERBS: &[&str] = &[\n100\t    \"read\", \"search\", \"query\", \"get\", \"list\", \"status\", \"recall\", \"observe\", \"answer\",\n101\t    \"view\", \"show\", \"find\", \"frame\", \"peek\", \"describe\", \"inspect\", \"lookup\",\n102\t];\n103\t\n104\t/// A0 — exact-name allowlist for the known read-only memory tools whose names are not verb-clean\n105\t/// (`graph.entity`, `episodic.range`, `threads.open`, `commitments.due`, `contradictions.pending`,\n106\t/// `ingest.status`). Matched on the whole lowercased tool name.\n107\tconst A0_TOOLS: &[&str] = &[\n108\t    \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n109\t    \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n110\t];\n111\t\n112\t/// EXACT-NAME input-authorization tables (Phase 8, WS-F gesture control). Input injection has NO\n113\t/// honest verb in the token sets (so it is fail-closed A3 by default). These exact `hid.*` names\n114\t/// authorize gesture-driven input at the correct tier — checked AFTER the danger-first A3/A2/A1\n115\t/// token pass, so a danger token ALWAYS wins (`hid.pointer.delete` → A3). Bare tokens (`move`/`type`/\n116\t/// `click`) are DELIBERATELY never added to the token sets, so `file.move`/`data.type` stay A3. The\n117\t/// keyboard tools avoid the token `key` (which is an A3 secret-target). Session-boundedness — A1\n118\t/// injection ONLY inside an owner-armed session — is enforced in Python (`gesture/session.py`), and\n119\t/// the raise-only registry (`registry.rs`) can only ever make an input tool MORE gated, never less.\n120\tconst INPUT_A1: &[&str] = &[\"hid.pointer.move\", \"hid.pointer.click\", \"hid.pointer.scroll\", \"hid.pointer.drag\"];\n121\tconst INPUT_A2: &[&str] = &[\"hid.type\", \"hid.combo\", \"hid.app.launch\"];\n122\t\n123\t/// Split a tool name into whole tokens on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/` and whitespace, lowercased.\n124\tfn tokens(tool: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n125\t    tool.split(|c: char| c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '/' || c.is_whitespace())\n126\t        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n127\t        .map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase())\n128\t        .collect()\n129\t}\n130\t\n131\t/// Classify a tool name into a tier. Danger FIRST, then A2, A1; A0 only via a positive safe-verb\n132\t/// or an exact safe-tool name; everything else (unknown / not-positively-safe / empty) → A3.\n133\tpub fn classify(tool: &str) -> Tier {\n134\t    let full = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n135\t    let tk = tokens(tool);\n136\t    if tk.is_empty() {\n137\t        return Tier::A3;\n138\t    }\n139\t    let has = |set: &[&str]| tk.iter().any(|t| set.contains(&t.as_str()));\n140\t    if has(A3_TOKENS) {\n141\t        return Tier::A3;\n142\t    }\n143\t    if has(A2_TOKENS) {\n144\t        return Tier::A2;\n145\t    }\n146\t    if has(A1_TOKENS) {\n147\t        return Tier::A1;\n148\t    }\n149\t    // Exact-name input tables — reached ONLY after the danger-first pass, so `hid.pointer.delete`\n150\t    // already returned A3. Bare `move`/`type` are never token-classified, so `file.move` stays A3.\n151\t    if INPUT_A1.contains(&full.as_str()) {\n152\t        return Tier::A1; // reversible pointer input (session-bounded in gesture/session.py)\n153\t    }\n154\t    if INPUT_A2.contains(&full.as_str()) {\n155\t        return Tier::A2; // keystrokes / combos / app-launch → queued for owner/device approval\n156\t    }\n157\t    if A0_TOOLS.contains(&full.as_str()) || has(A0_VERBS) {\n158\t        return Tier::A0;\n159\t    }\n160\t    Tier::A3 // not positively classified → fail-closed to the most-gated tier\n161\t}\n162\t\n163\tpub fn gate(tier: Tier) -> Decision {\n164\t    match tier {\n165\t        Tier::A0 | Tier::A1 => Decision::Auto,\n166\t        Tier::A2 => Decision::Queued,\n167\t        Tier::A3 => Decision::ExplicitRequired,\n168\t    }\n169\t}\n170\t\n171\t#[cfg(test)]\n172\tmod tests {\n173\t    use super::*;\n174\t\n175\t    #[test]\n176\t    fn classification_matches_spec_examples() {\n177\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n178\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.query\"), Tier::A0);\n179\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.write\"), Tier::A1);\n180\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"brief.compose\"), Tier::A1);\n181\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"email.send\"), Tier::A2);\n182\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"calendar.write\"), Tier::A2); // \"calendar\" (A2) beats \"write\" (A1)\n183\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"git.push\"), Tier::A3);\n184\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"deploy.prod\"), Tier::A3);\n185\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"data.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n186\t    }\n187\t\n188\t    #[test]\n189\t    fn unknown_and_empty_are_fail_closed_a3() {\n190\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"something.unclassified\"), Tier::A3);\n191\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"\"), Tier::A3);\n192\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"weird\"), Tier::A3);\n193\t        assert_eq!(gate(classify(\"something.unclassified\")), Decision::ExplicitRequired);\n194\t    }\n195\t\n196\t    #[test]\n197\t    fn substring_collisions_do_not_lower_the_tier() {\n198\t        // the exact review findings: a dangerous op must NOT ride a benign substring down a tier\n199\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"config.overwrite\"), Tier::A3, \"overwrite != write\");\n200\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"db.overwrite\"), Tier::A3);\n201\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"files.forget\"), Tier::A3, \"forget != get; fail-closed\");\n202\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.override\"), Tier::A3, \"financial + override\");\n203\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.get\"), Tier::A3, \"financial target is A3 regardless of verb\");\n204\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"iam.policy.write\"), Tier::A3, \"identity write is A3, not A1\");\n205\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"firewall.rule.write\"), Tier::A3);\n206\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"transaction.sign\"), Tier::A3, \"financial signing is A3, not A1\");\n207\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"secrets.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret read is A3, not A0\");\n208\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"credentials.get\"), Tier::A3);\n209\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"account.disable\"), Tier::A3);\n210\t    }\n211\t\n212\t    #[test]\n213\t    fn dangerous_ops_on_safe_targets_do_not_auto_run() {\n214\t        // the CRITICAL re-check finding: a target noun must NOT grant A0 to a dangerous verb\n215\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.encrypt\"), Tier::A3, \"ransomware-shaped op must NOT be A0\");\n216\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.destroy\"), Tier::A3);\n217\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"entity.merge\"), Tier::A3, \"irreversible rewrite → fail-closed, not A0\");\n218\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.export\"), Tier::A2, \"full-store egress must be queued, not auto\");\n219\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.dump\"), Tier::A2);\n220\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"snapshot.restore\"), Tier::A3, \"a restore reverts state → A3\");\n221\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"vault.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret-store read is A3 regardless of verb\");\n222\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"keyring.get\"), Tier::A3);\n223\t    }\n224\t\n225\t    #[test]\n226\t    fn safe_memory_tools_still_reach_a0() {\n227\t        for t in [\n228\t            \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n229\t            \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n230\t        ] {\n231\t            assert_eq!(classify(t), Tier::A0, \"{t} is a read-only memory tool → A0\");\n232\t        }\n233\t    }\n234\t\n235\t    #[test]\n236\t    fn danger_token_wins_over_safe_token_regardless_of_order() {\n237\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"read.then.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n238\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"delete.and.list\"), Tier::A3);\n239\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"search.and.deploy\"), Tier::A3);\n240\t    }\n241\t\n242\t    #[test]\n243\t    fn hid_input_tables_tier_correctly_and_danger_wins() {\n244\t        // exact HID input names authorize input at the right tier\n245\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.pointer.move\"), Tier::A1);\n246\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.pointer.click\"), Tier::A1);\n247\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.pointer.scroll\"), Tier::A1);\n248\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.pointer.drag\"), Tier::A1);\n249\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.type\"), Tier::A2, \"keystrokes are queued\");\n250\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.combo\"), Tier::A2);\n251\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.app.launch\"), Tier::A2, \"app-launch is queued\");\n252\t        // danger-first ALWAYS wins over an input name\n253\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.pointer.delete\"), Tier::A3, \"a danger token beats an input name\");\n254\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.pointer.sudo\"), Tier::A3);\n255\t        // unknown hid.* fails closed; the exact-name approach does NOT leak to token-named tools\n256\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"hid.unknown\"), Tier::A3, \"unknown hid.* → fail-closed A3\");\n257\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"file.move\"), Tier::A3, \"file.move is UNAFFECTED (never auto)\");\n258\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"data.type\"), Tier::A3, \"data.type is UNAFFECTED\");\n259\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"content.type\"), Tier::A3);\n260\t    }\n261\t\n262\t    #[test]\n263\t    fn gates_are_correct() {\n264\t        assert!(gate(Tier::A0).may_run());\n265\t        assert!(gate(Tier::A1).may_run());\n266\t        assert!(!gate(Tier::A2).may_run());\n267\t        assert!(!gate(Tier::A3).may_run());\n268\t    }\n269\t}\n270\t"}]},"uuid":"7dd242d9-9902-42c3-b6e0-727203abf7f6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:09.700Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e39abe71-78d9-40b9-8768-fd1455128ebe","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"fbd9f3ec-b0b2-4ab6-9448-c41675b89e7b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015r1yuGGJaywKc1GvPS3nJH","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Gesture value types (Phase 8, WS-F). A `Hand` is a 21-keypoint landmark set; a `GestureReading`\n2\tis the classifier's per-frame verdict (label + confidence + top1−top2 margin + pointer kinematics); a\n3\t`GestureIntent` is what the FSM decides to DO (still not an injection — the SessionGate authorizes).\"\"\"\n4\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n5\t\n6\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n7\tfrom typing import List, Tuple\n8\t\n9\t\n10\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n11\tclass Hand:\n12\t    landmarks: Tuple[Tuple[float, float, float], ...]   # 21 (x,y,z), normalized to the frame\n13\t    handedness: str = \"Right\"\n14\t    score: float = 1.0\n15\t\n16\t\n17\t# A HandObservation is a list[Hand] (empty = no hand in frame).\n18\t\n19\t\n20\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n21\tclass GestureReading:\n22\t    label: str                 # \"point\"|\"pinch\"|\"fist\"|\"open\"|\"neutral\"|\"swipe_left\"|\"swipe_right\"|\"spread\"|...\n23\t    confidence: float = 0.0\n24\t    dx: float = 0.0            # pointer kinematics (normalized) for the \"point\" driver\n25\t    dy: float = 0.0\n26\t    margin: float = 1.0        # top1 − top2 confidence margin (small ⇒ ambiguous ⇒ do nothing)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n30\tclass GestureIntent:\n31\t    kind: str                  # \"move\"|\"click\"|\"scroll_left\"|\"scroll_right\"|\"drag\"|\"type\"|\"launch\"|\"hand_lost\"|\"none\"\n32\t    dx: float = 0.0\n33\t    dy: float = 0.0\n34\t    arg: str = \"\"              # e.g. text to type / app to launch (owner-approved before it ever fires)\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef empty_observation() -> List[Hand]:\n38\t    return []\n39\t"}]},"uuid":"5b29cefc-d961-4e59-9c78-b23da3faeaf4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:10.184Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fbd9f3ec-b0b2-4ab6-9448-c41675b89e7b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6652aec9-82d6-4a30-85f7-7547dd6396ce","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018HmKUiB7fGDHdtfonfkPsD","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Gesture component Protocols + zero-dependency deterministic doubles (Phase 8, WS-F) — mirrors\n2\t`voice/components.py`. Every FSM/session/gate test runs against these scripted doubles: no camera, no\n3\tmodel, no OS input. The real providers (`landmark.OnnxHandLandmarker`, `classifier.*`, the platform\n4\t`InputBackend`s) live behind the same Protocols and lazy-import their heavy deps.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n8\t\n9\tfrom .types import GestureReading, Hand\n10\t\n11\t\n12\t@runtime_checkable\n13\tclass LandmarkModel(Protocol):\n14\t    egresses: bool                                    # on-box models are False; the loop refuses egressing ones\n15\t    def detect(self, image) -> List[Hand]: ...        # 21-keypoint hands, or [] (honest empty)\n16\t\n17\t\n18\t@runtime_checkable\n19\tclass GestureModel(Protocol):\n20\t    def classify(self, hands: List[Hand]) -> GestureReading: ...\n21\t\n22\t\n23\t@runtime_checkable\n24\tclass InputBackend(Protocol):\n25\t    def move(self, dx: float, dy: float) -> None: ...\n26\t    def click(self, button: str = \"left\") -> None: ...\n27\t    def scroll(self, dx: int, dy: int) -> None: ...\n28\t    def type(self, text: str) -> None: ...\n29\t    def combo(self, keys: str) -> None: ...\n30\t\n31\t\n32\t# --- deterministic doubles ------------------------------------------------------------------------\n33\tclass ScriptedLandmarker:\n34\t    \"\"\"Ignores the image; replays a scripted sequence of hand observations.\"\"\"\n35\t    egresses = False\n36\t\n37\t    def __init__(self, sequence: List[List[Hand]]):\n38\t        self._seq = list(sequence)\n39\t        self._i = 0\n40\t\n41\t    def detect(self, image) -> List[Hand]:\n42\t        if self._i >= len(self._seq):\n43\t            return []\n44\t        obs = self._seq[self._i]\n45\t        self._i += 1\n46\t        return obs\n47\t\n48\t\n49\tclass ScriptedGestures:\n50\t    \"\"\"Replays a scripted sequence of GestureReadings (bypasses the model).\"\"\"\n51\t    def __init__(self, readings: List[GestureReading]):\n52\t        self._seq = list(readings)\n53\t        self._i = 0\n54\t\n55\t    def classify(self, hands: List[Hand]) -> GestureReading:\n56\t        if self._i >= len(self._seq):\n57\t            return GestureReading(\"neutral\", 0.0)\n58\t        r = self._seq[self._i]\n59\t        self._i += 1\n60\t        return r\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tclass RecordingInputBackend:\n64\t    \"\"\"Records every injection call; touches NO OS. The double every gate test drives.\"\"\"\n65\t    def __init__(self):\n66\t        self.calls: List[tuple] = []\n67\t\n68\t    def move(self, dx, dy): self.calls.append((\"move\", dx, dy))\n69\t    def click(self, button=\"left\"): self.calls.append((\"click\", button))\n70\t    def scroll(self, dx, dy): self.calls.append((\"scroll\", dx, dy))\n71\t    def type(self, text): self.calls.append((\"type\", text))\n72\t    def combo(self, keys): self.calls.append((\"combo\", keys))\n73\t"}]},"uuid":"99b7c8f2-e8c7-4c1e-97ae-0c36c391cdf5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:10.502Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6652aec9-82d6-4a30-85f7-7547dd6396ce","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3e5471ee-821b-4dd0-a5f1-7db227a4c7d1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01329uxMDx2EGu82VnQykPq5","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Hand-landmark provider (Phase 8, WS-F F3) — the on-box deep-learning stage, behind the\n2\t`components.LandmarkModel` Protocol (mirrors `perception.vision.VisionModel`). `OnnxHandLandmarker`\n3\tlazy-imports the vendored `onnxruntime` and runs a 21-keypoint model at `~/.sigil/models/\n4\thand_landmark.onnx` (checksum-pinned); it is `egresses=False` (on-box) so the control loop\n5\tstructurally refuses an egressing model, and returns [] (honest empty) on any failure — never\n6\tfabricated landmarks. The `ScriptedLandmarker` double (in `components.py`) drives every test.\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom pathlib import Path\n10\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n11\t\n12\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n13\tfrom .types import Hand\n14\t\n15\t_MODEL = SIGIL_HOME / \"models\" / \"hand_landmark.onnx\"\n16\t\n17\t\n18\tclass OnnxHandLandmarker:\n19\t    egresses = False\n20\t\n21\t    def __init__(self, model_path: Optional[str] = None):\n22\t        self.model_path = Path(model_path) if model_path else _MODEL\n23\t        self._session = None\n24\t\n25\t    def _sess(self):\n26\t        if self._session is None:\n27\t            try:\n28\t                import onnxruntime as ort\n29\t                if not self.model_path.exists():\n30\t                    return None\n31\t                self._session = ort.InferenceSession(str(self.model_path),\n32\t                                                     providers=ort.get_available_providers())\n33\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no runtime/model → honest empty\n34\t                return None\n35\t        return self._session\n36\t\n37\t    def detect(self, image) -> List[Hand]:\n38\t        sess = self._sess()\n39\t        if sess is None or image is None:\n40\t            return []\n41\t        try:\n42\t            import numpy as np\n43\t            # NOTE: a wired model preprocesses (resize/normalize) + parses the palm-ROI → 21-kp output.\n44\t            # Kept deliberately minimal here: with no bundled .onnx this path returns [] (honest gap),\n45\t            # exactly like capture.grab_camera returning None rather than a fabricated frame.\n46\t            _ = np  # preprocessing lives here in a bundled build\n47\t            return []\n48\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n49\t            return []\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"860d1cce-3f3f-4276-bdfc-b64b21a7e8b8","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:16.762Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3e5471ee-821b-4dd0-a5f1-7db227a4c7d1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"94ebc5f0-105d-4eba-8c3d-4eae91c933a5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PS2PwB5GzsUBdxJahhxD9h","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The live gesture-control daemon (Phase 8, WS-F F7) — camera stream → landmarks → classify → FSM →\n2\tSessionGate, mirroring `voice/run.py run_mic`. Arms the session on start (indicator lit), disarms in a\n3\t`finally` (indicator cleared even on crash). Graceful fallbacks; components are injectable so the\n4\twhole loop runs deterministically on scripted doubles with no camera/model/OS input.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom .pipeline import GesturePipeline\n10\tfrom .session import SessionGate\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tdef run_gesture(*, store=None, owner_key=None, source=None, landmarker=None, classifier=None,\n14\t                backend=None, pipeline=None, gate=None, max_frames: Optional[int] = None) -> int:\n15\t    \"\"\"Run the loop; returns the number of frames processed. Injectable for tests.\"\"\"\n16\t    if store is None:\n17\t        from ..spine.store import SpineStore\n18\t        store = SpineStore()\n19\t    if source is None:\n20\t        from ..perception.camera_stream import CameraStreamSource\n21\t        source = CameraStreamSource()\n22\t    if landmarker is None:\n23\t        from .landmark import OnnxHandLandmarker\n24\t        landmarker = OnnxHandLandmarker()\n25\t    if classifier is None:\n26\t        from .features import RuleClassifier\n27\t        classifier = RuleClassifier()\n28\t    if backend is None:\n29\t        from ..platform import input_backend\n30\t        backend = input_backend()\n31\t    pipe = pipeline or GesturePipeline()\n32\t    g = gate or SessionGate(store, backend, owner_key=owner_key)\n33\t    g.arm(owner_key=owner_key)\n34\t    n = 0\n35\t    try:\n36\t        for frame in source.frames():\n37\t            reading = classifier.classify(landmarker.detect(frame))\n38\t            intent = pipe.on_frame(reading)\n39\t            if intent is not None:\n40\t                g.handle(intent)\n41\t            n += 1\n42\t            if max_frames is not None and n >= max_frames:\n43\t                break\n44\t    finally:\n45\t        g.disarm()\n46\t    return n\n47\t"}]},"uuid":"0c3fb389-fb94-492c-8109-c9dfc5c659bb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:17.300Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"94ebc5f0-105d-4eba-8c3d-4eae91c933a5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"20d0af36-06fe-41d6-85c9-bc48dbd000ce","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QJpy9S6HL3sYAdbWEEdjdG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL-HAND (Phase 8, WS-F) — fast, owner-armed hand-gesture control: a device camera → on-box\n2\tdeep-learning hand tracking → a debounced intent FSM → WARDEN-gated input injection. Observes the\n3\towner's OWN hand and controls the owner's OWN device — offense-free. Injection happens ONLY inside an\n4\towner-armed session; discrete actions (type/launch) are queued for approval; a gesture can never type\n5\ta password or launch an app on its own.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n7\t\n8\tassert_no_offense()\n9\t\n10\tfrom .pipeline import GesturePipeline  # noqa: E402\n11\tfrom .session import Session, SessionGate  # noqa: E402\n12\tfrom .types import GestureIntent, GestureReading, Hand  # noqa: E402\n13\t\n14\t__all__ = [\"GesturePipeline\", \"SessionGate\", \"Session\", \"Hand\", \"GestureReading\", \"GestureIntent\"]\n15\t"}]},"uuid":"00dbfb8b-ee36-45f4-a0f4-d39f15c1e0c3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:17.870Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"20d0af36-06fe-41d6-85c9-bc48dbd000ce","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"00dbfb8b-ee36-45f4-a0f4-d39f15c1e0c3","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a970854d297d55219","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAEkdFFoL5jfA76HBmiE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AX1k1jhj7GKoXRFeiYynP6","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/camera_stream.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8259,"cache_read_input_tokens":31926,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8259,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAEkbjin5NqFrjMYmruB","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"fefead66-5ae5-4753-9eef-a93c0dbbd9b3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:18.749Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"fefead66-5ae5-4753-9eef-a93c0dbbd9b3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AX1k1jhj7GKoXRFeiYynP6","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Persistent low-latency camera stream (Phase 8, WS-F F2) — a `voice.backends.MicAudioSource`-style\n2\tgenerator over ONE warm `ffmpeg` rawvideo pipe. Unlike `capture.grab_camera` (which cold-starts an\n3\tffmpeg process + writes a PNG + OCRs PER FRAME — hundreds of ms, unusable for control), this keeps the\n4\tdevice handle open and reads raw RGB frames off stdout. Drop-to-latest so inference never queues\n5\tbehind a slow frame. Honest-empty (no frames) if ffmpeg/device/numpy absent — never a fabricated\n6\tframe. `grab_camera` is left untouched (backward-compatible single-shot).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport shutil\n10\timport subprocess\n11\tfrom pathlib import Path\n12\tfrom typing import Iterator, List, Optional\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tclass CameraStreamSource:\n16\t    def __init__(self, device: str = \"/dev/video0\", width: int = 640, height: int = 480, fps: int = 30):\n17\t        self.device, self.width, self.height, self.fps = device, width, height, fps\n18\t\n19\t    def available(self) -> bool:\n20\t        return bool(shutil.which(\"ffmpeg\")) and Path(self.device).exists()\n21\t\n22\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator:\n23\t        \"\"\"Yield raw RGB frames (numpy HxWx3 uint8) from a single warm ffmpeg process. Ends silently\n24\t        if unavailable.\"\"\"\n25\t        if not self.available():\n26\t            return\n27\t        try:\n28\t            import numpy as np\n29\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no numpy → no stream (honest)\n30\t            return\n31\t        frame_bytes = self.width * self.height * 3\n32\t        proc: Optional[subprocess.Popen] = None\n33\t        try:\n34\t            proc = subprocess.Popen(\n35\t                [\"ffmpeg\", \"-loglevel\", \"quiet\", \"-f\", \"v4l2\", \"-i\", self.device,\n36\t                 \"-f\", \"rawvideo\", \"-pix_fmt\", \"rgb24\", \"-s\", f\"{self.width}x{self.height}\",\n37\t                 \"-r\", str(self.fps), \"pipe:1\"],\n38\t                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)\n39\t            while True:\n40\t                buf = proc.stdout.read(frame_bytes)\n41\t                if not buf or len(buf) < frame_bytes:\n42\t                    break\n43\t                yield np.frombuffer(buf, dtype=np.uint8).reshape((self.height, self.width, 3))\n44\t        except (OSError, ValueError):\n45\t            return\n46\t        finally:\n47\t            if proc is not None:\n48\t                proc.terminate()\n49\t                try:\n50\t                    proc.wait(timeout=2)\n51\t                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n52\t                    proc.kill()\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tclass ScriptedFrameSource:\n56\t    \"\"\"Deterministic double — yields a fixed list of frames (ndarrays or any placeholder). No device.\"\"\"\n57\t    def __init__(self, frames: List):\n58\t        self._frames = list(frames)\n59\t\n60\t    def available(self) -> bool:\n61\t        return True\n62\t\n63\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator:\n64\t        yield from self._frames\n65\t"}]},"uuid":"cd906177-e8b5-4a7e-b1ff-14c15c57860f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:18.770Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fefead66-5ae5-4753-9eef-a93c0dbbd9b3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"cd906177-e8b5-4a7e-b1ff-14c15c57860f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a970854d297d55219","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAEmGrCQuSicmLJZJWeE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the platform backends and the mesh registry."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3627,"cache_read_input_tokens":40185,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3627,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAEmFaJWvDvYjgSmALo3","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"c5e42fa3-651b-4893-a4f7-f0252458abb9","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:23.663Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"c5e42fa3-651b-4893-a4f7-f0252458abb9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a970854d297d55219","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAEmGrCQuSicmLJZJWeE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Sc9UejXWwfRPQUeb9HfdiN","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/platform/input.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3627,"cache_read_input_tokens":40185,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3627,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAEmFaJWvDvYjgSmALo3","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"c95fd35d-4d5f-419a-b368-25d748cfdf46","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:24.381Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"c95fd35d-4d5f-419a-b368-25d748cfdf46","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Sc9UejXWwfRPQUeb9HfdiN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-OS input-injection backends (Phase 8, WS-F F5) — implement `gesture.components.InputBackend`.\n2\tHeavy/native deps are detected/lazy so the package imports everywhere; a backend with no available\n3\tmethod is honest-inert (no-ops) rather than fabricating success. `input_backend()` selects by\n4\t`sys.platform`. Injection is ALWAYS routed through the SessionGate (a live armed session + the WARDEN\n5\ttier) — a backend is never called directly on an auto path.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\timport shutil\n9\timport subprocess\n10\timport sys\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tdef _run(argv) -> None:\n14\t    try:\n15\t        subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, timeout=5)\n16\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n17\t        pass\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass LinuxInputBackend:\n21\t    \"\"\"Prefers `ydotool` (Wayland-capable) then X11 `xdotool`; inert if neither is present.\"\"\"\n22\t    def __init__(self):\n23\t        self.method = \"ydotool\" if shutil.which(\"ydotool\") else (\"xdotool\" if shutil.which(\"xdotool\") else None)\n24\t\n25\t    def available(self) -> bool:\n26\t        return self.method is not None\n27\t\n28\t    def move(self, dx, dy):\n29\t        if self.method == \"xdotool\":\n30\t            _run([\"xdotool\", \"mousemove_relative\", \"--\", str(int(dx)), str(int(dy))])\n31\t        elif self.method == \"ydotool\":\n32\t            _run([\"ydotool\", \"mousemove\", \"-x\", str(int(dx)), \"-y\", str(int(dy))])\n33\t\n34\t    def click(self, button=\"left\"):\n35\t        if self.method == \"xdotool\":\n36\t            _run([\"xdotool\", \"click\", {\"left\": \"1\", \"middle\": \"2\", \"right\": \"3\"}.get(button, \"1\")])\n37\t        elif self.method == \"ydotool\":\n38\t            _run([\"ydotool\", \"click\", \"0xC0\"])\n39\t\n40\t    def scroll(self, dx, dy):\n41\t        if self.method == \"xdotool\":\n42\t            _run([\"xdotool\", \"click\", \"5\" if dx > 0 else \"4\"])\n43\t        elif self.method == \"ydotool\":\n44\t            _run([\"ydotool\", \"mousemove\", \"-w\", \"-y\", str(int(dx))])\n45\t\n46\t    def type(self, text):\n47\t        if self.method == \"xdotool\":\n48\t            _run([\"xdotool\", \"type\", \"--\", text])\n49\t        elif self.method == \"ydotool\":\n50\t            _run([\"ydotool\", \"type\", text])\n51\t\n52\t    def combo(self, keys):\n53\t        if self.method == \"xdotool\":\n54\t            _run([\"xdotool\", \"key\", keys])\n55\t        elif self.method == \"ydotool\":\n56\t            _run([\"ydotool\", \"key\", keys])\n57\t\n58\t\n59\tclass _SeamInputBackend:\n60\t    \"\"\"A documented native seam (macOS CGEvent via pyobjc / Windows SendInput via ctypes). Inert until\n61\t    the native path is wired — honest no-op, never a fabricated injection.\"\"\"\n62\t    native = \"\"\n63\t\n64\t    def available(self) -> bool:\n65\t        return False\n66\t\n67\t    def move(self, dx, dy): pass\n68\t    def click(self, button=\"left\"): pass\n69\t    def scroll(self, dx, dy): pass\n70\t    def type(self, text): pass\n71\t    def combo(self, keys): pass\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tclass MacOSInputBackend(_SeamInputBackend):\n75\t    native = \"CGEvent (pyobjc); requires Accessibility permission\"\n76\t\n77\t\n78\tclass WindowsInputBackend(_SeamInputBackend):\n79\t    native = \"SendInput (ctypes)\"\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef input_backend():\n83\t    if sys.platform == \"darwin\":\n84\t        return MacOSInputBackend()\n85\t    if sys.platform.startswith(\"win\"):\n86\t        return WindowsInputBackend()\n87\t    return LinuxInputBackend()\n88\t\n89\t\n90\tdef has_hid_inject() -> bool:\n91\t    b = input_backend()\n92\t    return b.available()\n93\t"}]},"uuid":"046c0aad-3cf7-4d43-a69a-555e8e33a5c9","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:24.410Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c95fd35d-4d5f-419a-b368-25d748cfdf46","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"046c0aad-3cf7-4d43-a69a-555e8e33a5c9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a970854d297d55219","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAEmGrCQuSicmLJZJWeE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013C32e5Y6GHqy7TjjURX1up","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/platform/base.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3627,"cache_read_input_tokens":40185,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3627,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAEmFaJWvDvYjgSmALo3","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"f5974595-b13e-4369-9c03-63ae9adc2e78","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:25.087Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"f5974595-b13e-4369-9c03-63ae9adc2e78","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013C32e5Y6GHqy7TjjURX1up","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The host-backend contract (Phase 7, WS-D). A `HostBackend` captures screen/camera into the same\n2\t`Frame` the perception stack already consumes, and advertises what the host can do. The capability\n3\tdescriptor is what the mesh routes on (see `sigil.mesh`): which host has a camera, a GPU for a local\n4\tVLM, and is always-on — so vision/compute go to the best-capable ONLINE host while authority stays\n5\tin the one signed spine.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n9\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..perception.capture import Frame\n12\t\n13\t\n14\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n15\tclass CapabilityDescriptor:\n16\t    host_id: str\n17\t    os: str\n18\t    has_screen: bool\n19\t    has_camera: bool\n20\t    has_gpu_vlm: bool          # a local moondream-class VLM is reachable (Ollama up)\n21\t    always_on: bool\n22\t    has_hid_inject: bool = False    # this host can inject cursor/keyboard events (WS-F gesture control)\n23\t    has_camera_stream: bool = False # this host can stream the camera (persistent, low-latency)\n24\t\n25\t    def to_dict(self) -> dict:\n26\t        return {\"host_id\": self.host_id, \"os\": self.os, \"has_screen\": self.has_screen,\n27\t                \"has_camera\": self.has_camera, \"has_gpu_vlm\": self.has_gpu_vlm, \"always_on\": self.always_on,\n28\t                \"has_hid_inject\": self.has_hid_inject, \"has_camera_stream\": self.has_camera_stream}\n29\t\n30\t\n31\t@runtime_checkable\n32\tclass HostBackend(Protocol):\n33\t    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]: ...\n34\t    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]: ...\n35\t    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor: ...\n36\t\n37\t\n38\t# --- shared, OS-agnostic probes ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n39\tdef host_id() -> str:\n40\t    \"\"\"A stable per-host id (persisted once under SIGIL_HOME). Identifies this host in the mesh.\"\"\"\n41\t    import uuid\n42\t\n43\t    from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n44\t    f = SIGIL_HOME / \"host_id\"\n45\t    try:\n46\t        v = f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n47\t        if v:\n48\t            return v\n49\t    except OSError:\n50\t        pass\n51\t    SIGIL_HOME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n52\t    hid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]\n53\t    try:\n54\t        f.write_text(hid, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n55\t    except OSError:\n56\t        pass\n57\t    return hid\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef probe_gpu_vlm() -> bool:\n61\t    \"\"\"True iff a local moondream-class VLM endpoint (Ollama) is reachable — cheap TCP probe.\"\"\"\n62\t    import os\n63\t    import socket\n64\t    from urllib.parse import urlparse\n65\t    u = urlparse(os.environ.get(\"OLLAMA_HOST\", \"http://127.0.0.1:11434\"))\n66\t    try:\n67\t        with socket.create_connection((u.hostname or \"127.0.0.1\", u.port or 11434), timeout=0.5):\n68\t            return True\n69\t    except OSError:\n70\t        return False\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef probe_always_on() -> bool:\n74\t    import os\n75\t    return os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ALWAYS_ON\", \"\").lower() in (\"1\", \"true\", \"yes\")\n76\t"}]},"uuid":"c5f15b1f-0346-4b45-9aa8-ac2ca45c0df6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:25.111Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f5974595-b13e-4369-9c03-63ae9adc2e78","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3f643197-34ce-4e81-95ff-a756a7c4358f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GWeutvMpzg1ZfEEZob6PQB","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Linux backend (Phase 7, WS-D) — wraps the existing perception capture verbatim (behaviour-\n2\tpreserving; the whole stack above is unchanged).\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport shutil\n6\tfrom pathlib import Path\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom ..perception.capture import Frame, grab_camera, grab_screen\n10\tfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, host_id, probe_always_on, probe_gpu_vlm\n11\t\n12\t_SCREEN_TOOLS = (\"scrot\", \"gnome-screenshot\", \"spectacle\", \"import\")\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tclass LinuxBackend:\n16\t    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n17\t        return grab_screen()\n18\t\n19\t    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n20\t        return grab_camera()\n21\t\n22\t    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor:\n23\t        from .input import has_hid_inject\n24\t        cam = Path(\"/dev/video0\").exists()\n25\t        return CapabilityDescriptor(\n26\t            host_id=host_id(), os=\"linux\",\n27\t            has_screen=any(shutil.which(t) for t in _SCREEN_TOOLS),\n28\t            has_camera=cam, has_gpu_vlm=probe_gpu_vlm(), always_on=probe_always_on(),\n29\t            has_hid_inject=has_hid_inject(), has_camera_stream=cam and bool(shutil.which(\"ffmpeg\")))\n30\t"}]},"uuid":"e67f79a0-b551-4bcc-b627-7612ce378048","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:25.597Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3f643197-34ce-4e81-95ff-a756a7c4358f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Offense-free.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\timport sys\n8\t\n9\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n10\tfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, HostBackend\n11\t\n12\tassert_no_offense()\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef input_backend():\n16\t    \"\"\"The InputBackend for this OS (WS-F gesture injection). Always routed through the SessionGate.\"\"\"\n17\t    from .input import input_backend as _ib\n18\t    return _ib()\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef host() -> HostBackend:\n22\t    \"\"\"The backend for this OS (selected by `sys.platform`).\"\"\"\n23\t    if sys.platform == \"darwin\":\n24\t        from .macos import MacOSBackend\n25\t        return MacOSBackend()\n26\t    if sys.platform.startswith(\"win\"):\n27\t        from .windows import WindowsBackend\n28\t        return WindowsBackend()\n29\t    from .linux import LinuxBackend\n30\t    return LinuxBackend()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\t__all__ = [\"host\", \"input_backend\", \"HostBackend\", \"CapabilityDescriptor\"]\n34\t"}]},"uuid":"f681735b-4fbb-410c-bebe-b105f509da66","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:25.763Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"aa9f037a-a7b5-482a-a357-db83445f71fc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
