{"parentUuid":"006fdd43-a226-4557-a37a-61b82132f124","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012Yrd3efSQEefu464ghRqyf","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\",\n27\t             \"has_hid_inject\", \"has_camera_stream\")\n28\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n29\t\n30\t\n31\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n32\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n33\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n34\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n35\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"]),\n36\t            \"has_hid_inject\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_hid_inject\", False)),\n37\t            \"has_camera_stream\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_camera_stream\", False))}\n38\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n39\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n43\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n44\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n45\t    latest: dict = {}\n46\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n47\t        p = r.payload\n48\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n49\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n50\t    return latest\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n54\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n55\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n56\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n57\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n61\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n62\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n63\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n67\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n68\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n69\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n70\t    state: dict = {}\n71\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n72\t        p = r.payload\n73\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n74\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n75\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n76\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tclass DeviceApprover:\n80\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n81\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n82\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n83\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n84\t        self.store = store\n85\t        self.device_key = device_key\n86\t\n87\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n88\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n89\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n90\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n91\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n92\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n93\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"9fe259ee-4d4e-4cf7-bc4b-2b1eadb915e5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:32:30.347Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"006fdd43-a226-4557-a37a-61b82132f124","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f63cf95f-4a9d-4aed-90ff-eb2f70aa3b7e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XNTQzk8DyFSC4wJw2pyEfB","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL Phase 8 WS-F — SIGIL-HAND gesture control: debounced fail-safe FSM, invariant landmark\n2\tfeatures, and THE KEYSTONE — injection only inside an owner-armed session, A2 actions queued (a\n3\tgesture can never type a password or launch an app). Run: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python tests/test_gesture.py\"\"\"\n4\timport math\n5\timport tempfile\n6\tfrom pathlib import Path\n7\t\n8\tfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\n9\tfrom sigil.gesture import GesturePipeline, Session, SessionGate\n10\tfrom sigil.gesture.components import RecordingInputBackend, ScriptedGestures, ScriptedLandmarker\n11\tfrom sigil.gesture.features import RuleClassifier, invariant_features\n12\tfrom sigil.gesture.run import run_gesture\n13\tfrom sigil.gesture.types import GestureIntent, GestureReading, Hand\n14\tfrom sigil.perception.camera_stream import ScriptedFrameSource\n15\tfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\n16\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tOWNER = generate_keypair()\n19\tOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tdef _store():\n23\t    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tclass FakeCls:\n27\t    \"\"\"hid.pointer.* → A1 (inject); everything else → A2 (queue). Deterministic stand-in for the oracle.\"\"\"\n28\t    def classify(self, tool):\n29\t        return Tier.A1 if tool.startswith(\"hid.pointer\") else Tier.A2\n30\t\n31\t\n32\t# ---- F0 FSM: debounce + fail-safe ----------------------------------------------------------------\n33\tdef test_fsm_requires_confirm_frames_and_is_failsafe_on_ambiguity():\n34\t    p = GesturePipeline(confirm_frames=3, margin_min=0.15, conf_min=0.6)\n35\t    hi = GestureReading(\"pinch\", 0.9, margin=0.5)\n36\t    assert p.on_frame(hi) is None and p.on_frame(hi) is None, \"one/two frames fire nothing (hysteresis)\"\n37\t    fired = p.on_frame(hi)\n38\t    assert fired and fired.kind == \"click\", \"3 consecutive confident pinches fire a click\"\n39\t    # low margin → treated as neutral → nothing fires, streak resets\n40\t    p2 = GesturePipeline(confirm_frames=2, margin_min=0.2)\n41\t    p2.on_frame(GestureReading(\"pinch\", 0.9, margin=0.5))\n42\t    assert p2.on_frame(GestureReading(\"pinch\", 0.9, margin=0.05)) is None, \"an ambiguous (low-margin) frame does nothing\"\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef test_fsm_hand_lost_disarms_and_point_moves():\n46\t    p = GesturePipeline(hand_lost_frames=2)\n47\t    assert p.on_frame(None) is None\n48\t    hl = p.on_frame(None)\n49\t    assert hl and hl.kind == \"hand_lost\", \"sustained no-hand emits a disarm signal\"\n50\t    p2 = GesturePipeline(deadzone=0.001)\n51\t    mv = p2.on_frame(GestureReading(\"point\", 0.9, dx=0.5, dy=0.2, margin=0.4))\n52\t    assert mv and mv.kind == \"move\" and mv.dx == 0.5, \"a point pose drives pointer movement\"\n53\t    assert p2.on_frame(GestureReading(\"point\", 0.9, dx=0.0, dy=0.0, margin=0.4)) is None, \"sub-deadzone → no move\"\n54\t\n55\t\n56\t# ---- F4 feature invariance -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n57\tdef test_features_are_translation_scale_rotation_invariant():\n58\t    base = Hand(tuple((math.cos(i) * 0.1 + 0.5, math.sin(i) * 0.1 + 0.5, 0.0) for i in range(21)))\n59\t    f0 = invariant_features(base)\n60\t    translated = Hand(tuple((x + 0.3, y - 0.2, z) for x, y, z in base.landmarks))\n61\t    scaled = Hand(tuple((x * 2.0, y * 2.0, z) for x, y, z in base.landmarks))\n62\t    th, c, s = 0.7, math.cos(0.7), math.sin(0.7)\n63\t    rotated = Hand(tuple((x * c - y * s, x * s + y * c, z) for x, y, z in base.landmarks))\n64\t    for h, name in ((translated, \"translate\"), (scaled, \"scale\"), (rotated, \"rotate\")):\n65\t        f = invariant_features(h)\n66\t        assert max(abs(a - b) for a, b in zip(f0, f)) < 1e-6, f\"features must be invariant to {name}\"\n67\t\n68\t\n69\tdef test_rule_classifier_reads_poses():\n70\t    fist = Hand(tuple((0.5, 0.5, 0) for _ in range(21)))            # all tips near wrist → fist\n71\t    assert RuleClassifier().classify([fist]).label in (\"fist\", \"pinch\")\n72\t    assert RuleClassifier().classify([]).label == \"neutral\", \"no hand → neutral\"\n73\t\n74\t\n75\t# ---- F6 THE KEYSTONE: armed session + tier gate --------------------------------------------------\n76\tdef test_injection_only_inside_an_armed_session():\n77\t    s = _store(); b = RecordingInputBackend()\n78\t    g = SessionGate(s, b, classifier=FakeCls())\n79\t    r = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=3, dy=2))\n80\t    assert r[\"injected\"] is False and \"no armed session\" in r[\"reason\"] and not b.calls, \"no session → NO injection\"\n81\t    g.arm(owner_key=OWNER)\n82\t    r = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=3, dy=2))\n83\t    assert r[\"injected\"] is True and (\"move\", 3, 2) in b.calls, \"A1 pointer move injects within the session\"\n84\t    g.disarm()\n85\t    assert g.handle(GestureIntent(\"click\"))[\"injected\"] is False, \"disarm stops injection\"\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tdef test_a2_gesture_actions_queue_never_inject():\n89\t    s = _store(); b = RecordingInputBackend()\n90\t    g = SessionGate(s, b, classifier=FakeCls())\n91\t    g.arm(owner_key=OWNER)\n92\t    r = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"type\", arg=\"my-password\"))\n93\t    assert r[\"injected\"] is False and \"queued\" in r, \"a keystroke gesture QUEUES, never auto-injects\"\n94\t    assert not any(c[0] == \"type\" for c in b.calls), \"a gesture can NEVER type a password on its own\"\n95\t    r2 = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"launch\", arg=\"terminal\"))\n96\t    assert r2[\"injected\"] is False and \"queued\" in r2, \"an app-launch gesture QUEUES for approval\"\n97\t    # the queued record binds to the exact action + carries no injected effect\n98\t    queued = [x for x in s.iter_records() if x.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"gesture.action\"]\n99\t    assert queued and queued[0].payload.get(\"action_token\") and queued[0].payload[\"status\"] == \"awaiting-approval\"\n100\t\n101\t\n102\tdef test_hand_lost_intent_disarms_the_session():\n103\t    s = _store(); b = RecordingInputBackend()\n104\t    g = SessionGate(s, b, classifier=FakeCls())\n105\t    g.arm(owner_key=OWNER)\n106\t    g.handle(GestureIntent(\"hand_lost\"))\n107\t    assert g.session is None, \"a hand-lost intent auto-disarms the session\"\n108\t    assert g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=1, dy=1))[\"injected\"] is False, \"and injection then stops\"\n109\t\n110\t\n111\t# ---- F2/F7 stream + end-to-end -------------------------------------------------------------------\n112\tdef test_run_gesture_end_to_end_with_doubles():\n113\t    s = _store(); b = RecordingInputBackend()\n114\t    readings = [GestureReading(\"pinch\", 0.9, margin=0.5)] * 3 + [GestureReading(\"point\", 0.9, dx=0.4, dy=0.1, margin=0.4)]\n115\t    n = run_gesture(store=s, owner_key=OWNER, source=ScriptedFrameSource([None] * 4),\n116\t                    landmarker=ScriptedLandmarker([[]] * 4), classifier=ScriptedGestures(readings), backend=b,\n117\t                    gate=SessionGate(s, b, classifier=FakeCls(), owner_key=OWNER),\n118\t                    pipeline=GesturePipeline(confirm_frames=3, deadzone=0.001))\n119\t    assert n == 4\n120\t    assert any(c[0] == \"click\" for c in b.calls), \"3 pinch frames fired a click end-to-end\"\n121\t    assert any(c[0] == \"move\" for c in b.calls), \"the point pose moved the cursor\"\n122\t    # the session was disarmed in the finally\n123\t    assert any(r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"gesture.session_disarmed\" for r in s.iter_records())\n124\t\n125\t\n126\t# ---- F5 capability flags -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n127\tdef test_capability_advertises_hid_flags():\n128\t    from sigil.mesh import advertise_capability, capability_map\n129\t    s = _store()\n130\t    advertise_capability(s, {\"host_id\": \"h\", \"os\": \"linux\", \"has_screen\": True, \"has_camera\": True,\n131\t                             \"has_gpu_vlm\": False, \"always_on\": True,\n132\t                             \"has_hid_inject\": True, \"has_camera_stream\": True}, OWNER)\n133\t    m = capability_map(s, OP)\n134\t    assert m[\"h\"][\"has_hid_inject\"] is True and m[\"h\"][\"has_camera_stream\"] is True\n135\t\n136\t\n137\t# ---- F1 WARDEN input tables (real oracle) --------------------------------------------------------\n138\tdef test_warden_input_tiers_and_danger_wins():\n139\t    from sigil.agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n140\t    k = Path(\"/home/kali/sigil/kernel/target/release/sigil-kernel\")\n141\t    if not k.exists():\n142\t        print(\"    (skip — kernel not built)\")\n143\t        return\n144\t    kc = KernelClassifier(kernel_bin=str(k))\n145\t    assert kc.classify(\"hid.pointer.move\") == Tier.A1 and kc.classify(\"hid.pointer.click\") == Tier.A1\n146\t    assert kc.classify(\"hid.type\") == Tier.A2 and kc.classify(\"hid.app.launch\") == Tier.A2\n147\t    assert kc.classify(\"hid.pointer.delete\") == Tier.A3, \"danger token beats an input name\"\n148\t    assert kc.classify(\"file.move\") == Tier.A3 and kc.classify(\"data.type\") == Tier.A3, \"token-named tools unaffected\"\n149\t\n150\t\n151\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n152\t    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith(\"test_\")]\n153\t    passed = 0\n154\t    for fn in {"parentUuid":"c4323a26-ef29-4b90-9756-699689f2294c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01K6BF72MCav4v3C9aLGU6Nz","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"KernelClassifier (Phase 7, P0.1) — the Python↔Rust WARDEN bridge. The authoritative danger\nclassifier is Rust `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` (token-based, danger-first, fail-closed to A3),\ncomposed with the raise-only registry pins in `Warden::decide`. Python's `Governor` otherwise\nTRUSTS a Proposal's declared tier; this bridge lets the mesh DERIVE a tool's tier from that\nfail-closed oracle instead of self-declaring it — so an Operator step or a Vision egress hop is\ntiered by the same code that enforces at the kernel.\n\nConsulting the kernel is a pure A0 observation (`sigil-kernel classify` writes NO action-log\nrecord). Any failure — missing binary, timeout, non-zero exit, unparseable output, or an unknown\ntier string — resolves to **A3** (fail-closed to the most-gated tier), mirroring the Rust\nclassifier's own posture: never let a bridge error silently downgrade a dangerous tool.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport subprocess\n\nfrom ..voice.dispatch import _default_bin  # reuse the exact kernel-binary resolution\nfrom .base import Tier\n\n_TIER = {\"A0\": Tier.A0, \"A1\": Tier.A1, \"A2\": Tier.A2, \"A3\": Tier.A3}\n\n\nclass KernelClassifier:\n    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 15):\n        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n        self.timeout = timeout\n\n    def classify(self, tool: str) -> Tier:\n        \"\"\"Return the WARDEN tier for `tool`. Fail-closed to A3 on ANY error/ambiguity.\"\"\"\n        if not tool or not tool.strip():\n            return Tier.A3\n        try:\n            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"classify\", tool, \"--json\"],\n                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n            return Tier.A3\n        if proc.returncode != 0:\n            return Tier.A3\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])\n            return _TIER.get(str(data.get(\"tier\")), Tier.A3)\n        except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError, TypeError):\n            return Tier.A3","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"55cead61-d074-4736-8085-75bcfd977089","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:33:35.370Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c4323a26-ef29-4b90-9756-699689f2294c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55-    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56-    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57-    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58-    mandate: str = \"\"\n59-    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60-\n61-    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"9406c89e-b259-4d6d-a94a-82d99143988a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:33:36.532Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8d865f38-0ceb-4cf9-8efd-98c80459c8e7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"55f6e39c-4a11-492c-8134-999c902baf0f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a970854d297d55219","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAEryd2NYemR7iYGMLfS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Wu3wVieH91kcp41TychteX","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && echo \"===== identity =====\" && grep -n \"def owner_keypair\\|def owner_pubkey\" sigil/governor/identity.py && echo \"===== assert_no_offense =====\" && grep -n \"def assert_no_offense\\|def sha256_hex\\|def sign\\|def generate_keypair\" sigil/reuse.py && echo \"===== _default_bin =====\" && grep -n \"def _default_bin\" -A 12 sigil/voice/dispatch.py","description":"Read identity, reuse helpers, default_bin"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6357,"cache_read_input_tokens":54892,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6357,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAErx51Uw2fKBcbLGe1q","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"33ea07ba-6d8c-4074-a609-18d2bbff54c6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:34:53.722Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"55f6e39c-4a11-492c-8134-999c902baf0f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011mm1kTkmUvWUyWbNsxsfEz","type":"tool_result","content":"===== authn.signed_payload / verify_signed =====\n\"\"\"Shared authentication for governance events (Phase 6). Every state-changing governance event\n(promotion grant/revoke, kill engage/release, approval approve/deny) carries an Ed25519 signature by\nthe OWNER key over its canonical authenticated CORE fields, plus the signer's pubkey. Verification is\nfail-closed: no trusted key, no signature, a pubkey that isn't the trusted owner key, or a tampered\ncore → NOT authentic. This is the primitive that makes \"zero unauthorized A2/A3\" provable from the\nlog rather than promised (a forged governance event never verifies, so it is ignored).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Sequence\n\nfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sign, verify_one\n\n\ndef _canon(core: dict) -> bytes:\n    m = canonical_json(core)\n    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n\n\ndef signed_payload(core: dict, owner_key) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"core = the authenticated fields (must be JSON-canonicalizable). Returns core + {sig, pubkey}.\n    With no owner key, sig/pubkey are None → the event will never verify (a caller that requires\n    authentication must reject it).\"\"\"\n    sig = sign(owner_key.private_key_b64, _canon(core)) if owner_key else None\n    return {**core, \"sig\": sig, \"pubkey\": owner_key.public_key_b64 if owner_key else None}\n\n\ndef verify_signed(payload: dict, core_fields: Sequence[str], trusted_pubkey: Optional[str]) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True iff `payload` carries a valid owner signature over its declared core fields. Fail-closed.\"\"\"\n    if not trusted_pubkey:\n        return False\n    sig = payload.get(\"sig\")\n    if not sig or payload.get(\"pubkey\") != trusted_pubkey:\n        return False\n    core = {k: payload.get(k) for k in core_fields}\n    return verify_one(trusted_pubkey, _canon(core), sig)","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"6b8eb8a2-8f05-4c52-97b3-85382ea5e9cc","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:34:54.378Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"55f6e39c-4a11-492c-8134-999c902baf0f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"18472f5e-3620-4c8c-841e-c84446169d74","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KaFtakpNwDD8r9APAqYPQg","type":"tool_result","content":"===== spine store append signature =====\n49:    def append(\n---\n    def append(\n        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n        ts: str | None = None,\n    ) -> int:\n        content = {\n            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n        }\n        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n        record = {\n            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n        }\n        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n        self._last = entry\n        return entry.seq\n\n    # {"parentUuid":"1cece7c4-8791-4106-9979-cc0cc0dc25a7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"15d22289-34bd-4b16-93d1-385f77b75827","agentId":"a970854d297d55219","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013vaJ3BfPxt3AWiE4khVH2C","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\nQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\nEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n\nRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n\nThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\ntransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import List, Optional\n\nfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\nfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n\nSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n\n\ndef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n\n\ndef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n    allowed = set()\n    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n    if extra_pubkeys:\n        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n        return False\n    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n\n\ndef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n    resolved = set()","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"83fdd072-444d-49e4-a345-b4510e80f9b3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T21:38:07.028Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1cece7c4-8791-4106-9979-cc0cc0dc25a7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
